==============================================================================
HUONG DAN SINH CAU HOI TEST
==============================================================================

Cac kieu cau hoi nen sinh (cau tra loi phai nam trong context tren):
1. Dinh nghia glossary term (dung ten chinh xac, vi du: 'ABC Classification la gi?')
2. Dinh nghia voi ten sai chinh ta (de test suggestion, vi du: 'ABV Classification la gi?')
3. Lineage: hoi upstream/downstream cua mot dataset (vi du: 'lineage cua dim_material')
4. Owner cua dataset (dung ten owner nhu 'Nguyen Thi Thu Hang...')
5. Schema/field cua dataset: ten field, kieu du lieu, nullable, primary key, foreign key
6. Lien ket term voi dataset (glossary_terms cua dataset / linked_datasets cua term)
7. Domain: 'dataset nao trong domain X?', 'co bao nhieu dataset trong domain X?'
8. Listing: 'liet ke datasets', 'liet ke glossary terms', 'co bao nhieu datasets?'
9. Cau hoi voi ten domain sai chinh ta (test suggestion domain)
10. Kiem tra ton tai: 'dataset X co ton tai khong?'
11. Ambiguity: dung 2 entity ten gan giong trong nhom similar_name_groups de test phan biet

Luu y:
- Chi sinh cau hoi ma cau tra loi co the tra cuu duoc tu context o tren.
- Su dung ten chinh xac cua dataset/term/domain nhu trong danh sach.
- Co the khop thu cong so luong (count) voi context.
